Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the cognitive object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 913. The moduli space has dimension at most 902 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to cognitive structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 913/902 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
